- **diff-viva**: Oral exam on a code change before you merge or adopt it. Use when someone is about to own code they did not fully write (agent output, a teammate's branch, a vendored patch) and says "quiz me on this diff", "make sure I understand this before I merge", "comprehension check", or "viva this PR". Picks risky anchors from the diff, asks one closed-book question at a time, marks answers strictly, and issues an OWNED / NOT_YET / INTENT_MISMATCH receipt. Not for finding bugs line by line, and not for learning a topic from scratch. ([fangdididi/diff-viva](https://github.com/fangdididi/diff-viva/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **diff-walkthrough**: Produce a guided walkthrough of a code diff, pull request, merge request, commit range, or staged changes. Use when the user asks to explain, narrate, summarize, review, or walk through a diff. Supports configurable depth and output formats, defaults to HTML, and uses Plannotator when available.
